# WF-16 — Study on the four-port birdcage

Item briefs and execution history (operator directive 2026-10-01). The §7 row is the status of record.

### §9 item 244 — 2026-10-05 (chain U4; depends on item 240)

Depends on item 240 landing on `main` with `WF-15` ✅; if it has not, skip this item.

*Drafted by a general-purpose agent for the 2026-10-05 03:00 daily review; the review reviews it.* Licence: §10 chain U4 (PROJECT_PLAN.md:4648–4650), a weekly-enumerated step (§9 rule (4)), serial on U3. No band, tolerance, Done-when or validation target moves. Every band below is imported by name from the file:line cited; none is set here.

**`WF-16` step 1: the `Study` object on the F-small four-port birdcage.** The `Study` class item 240 lands is extended, not duplicated. It builds item 192's ×1 fixture on exact port rectangles with κ off, solves it at 64 MHz, and returns S and a Touchstone file. Both must equal `ANS-4` step 5's registered exact-rectangle records at 1e-9, with `PORT-11`'s reciprocity / passivity / C4 gates imported and green. Its new public names are registered in `WF-14`'s envelope.
- **Executor:** implementer. It makes additive `src/` changes (new classes in item 240's `study.py`, plus private sheet-geometry helpers copied verbatim from tests), adds `__all__` exports, writes one new test module, and updates the registry and snapshot. No existing gate module, helper, record or `src/` function is edited.
- **Tier:** W0 standard (`WF-15`'s W1, verbatim). W1 heavy by ceiling (`-n 2`, container 400 s, the ceiling items 192 / 225 gave the same fixture at the same width). W2 heavy (`-n 8`, 590 s). W3 / W4 standard (the `WF-14` / `WF-13` windows, verbatim). Complex build for W0–W2. The row's "heavy by prediction" stands.
- **Class:** chain step (solve), measured median **11 min** (2026-10-05 03:00 restock table).
- **Cost:** ≈ 11 measured / ≈ 60 predicted min. That is 15 fixed + ≈ 30 code + windows ≈ 2 + 2.5 + 0.5 + 5 + 0.1 + 0.6 min + ≈ 4 spare. Tight against the 1-hour timebox. **If W1 is not green by minute 40, park** (rule (d)).
- **If item 240's landed API differs from its brief** (names, the result object, the solve/accessor split), the executor adapts the *names* in this brief to what landed. The *anchors, bands, records, ranks and windows* below are not adapted. It discloses the mapping in the journal.

**Extension points U4 adds to item 240's API** (names are proposals; the review or executor may rename them):
- (E1) **A four-port birdcage geometry object on exact port rectangles:** `FourPortBirdcageGeometry` (frozen dataclass).
  - It has a `f_small()` classmethod that returns item 192's ×1 fixture.
  - `.build(comm)` returns the mesh, cell tags, the narrowed sheet facet tags, and one record per port (tag, azimuth, `h`, `w = A/h`, area, facet count).
  - **The strip fraction is fixed at the SPEC's exact rectangle**, so no ragged route is offered (operator ruling 2026-10-02). It takes no κ argument.
  - The conductor (σ = `SIGMA_WIRE_S_PER_M`) is a geometry field. The phantom is the Study's `material` slot.
- (E2) **A lumped-port drive/sweep excitation:** `LumpedPortDrive(z0_ohm=50.0, port_impedance_ohm=50.0, drive_voltage_v=1+0j)`.
  - It builds the four `LumpedSheetPortSpec`s and `PortDefinition`s from the geometry's port records, field for field as the direct call does.
  - It runs `run_n_port_sparameter_sweep(..., degree=study.degree)`.
  - For this excitation the Study passes **no** Dirichlet field (zero tangential E).
- (E3) **An S + Touchstone result:** the solve result additionally exposes `s_matrix`, `z_matrix`, `sweep` (the `SParameterSweepResult`), `port_definitions` and `specs`, plus `.write_touchstone(path)`.
  - `.write_touchstone(path)` calls `export_touchstone([sweep], port_definitions, output_path=path, write_csv_companion=False)` **on rank 0 only**, then `Barrier`, and returns the path on every rank.
  - `.mean_sar()` raises on a port Study.
- (E4) **New public names `FourPortBirdcageGeometry` and `LumpedPortDrive`**, exported in `__all__` (item 240's 28 → 30). If item 240 exported its result class, the new attributes ride on it; otherwise nothing more is exported.

**Settled from the code (read before designing).**
- **The records.** `ANS4_EXACT_STRIP_S_P1_RECORDS` (`tests/validation/test_ans4_exact_strip_records.py:141–226`) is degree 1, κ off, ×1 (117 206 cells).
  - It was registered from `20261003T093408Z_ANS-4-item192-W1-p1.log:2117–2132` and is asserted at `-n 2` (`ANS4_EXACT_STRIP_RECORD_RANK_WIDTH`, `:227`).
  - In its own module it is held to `STEP3A_RECORD_RTOL` = 1e-6 (`test_ans4_resolution_ladder.py:198`).
  - `ANS4_EXACT_STRIP_S_P2_X1` (`:233–318`) is the **degree-2** record, measured at **`-n 8`** (`20261003T094036Z_ANS-4-item192-W3-p2.log:2199–2216`). It is "printed comparand only, never asserted" (`:229–232`).
  - **A degree-2 record exists, but only at `-n 8`. There is no degree-2 record at `-n 2`, and none is invented.** The `FEM_EM_ANS4_EXACT_DEGREE` knob has no default (`:13–14`, `:336–343`): unset, the module skips.
- **The 1e-9 band is `LEG_D0_REPRODUCTION_BAND`** (`tests/validation/test_port_birdcage_four_port.py:165`). It is this fixture family's print-precision reproduction band (`:131–135`). known-issues.md:80 names it as the tightest record band on a MUMPS solve in `tests/`. Imported, not restated.
- **Why 1e-9 is backed across processes.** MUMPS is not bitwise across processes at `-n 2` (known-issues.md:69, `OPS-43`: 1-ULP drift in 2 of 34 children).
  - The **same comparison** (fresh solve against the 10-digit record, Frobenius-relative) read **9.557e-11 at 64 MHz in two separate processes**: `20261004T093239Z_ANS-4-item212-W0-p1-unset.log:2172` and `20261004T183107Z_ANS-4-item225-W0-p1-unset.log:2172`. That is ≈ 10× under the band.
  - Against full-precision JSON the cross-process spread is 5.628e-16 / 8.256e-16 at degree 1, `-n 2` (`…item212-W0…:2179`, `…item225-W0…:2179`). It is **8.912e-13 at degree 2, `-n 8`** (`…item192-W3-p2.log:2300`).
  - So the record term is print rounding. A `.9e` print is ≤ 5e-10 relative per entry, hence ≤ 5e-10 in the Frobenius norm, by construction. Rounding plus solve spread is ≤ 5.01e-10 < 1e-9 at both degrees.
  - At degree 2 this is **reasoned, not yet measured**: `P2_X1` has never been asserted against a fresh process.
- **The direct call** is the exact-strip module's base rung, verbatim (`:442–448`): `_four_port_rung(name, np.zeros(4), frequency_hz=FREQUENCY_64_HZ, degree=d, leg_sheet_strip_fraction=GATED_WIDTH_FRACTION)`.
  - Where things are defined: `test_port_birdcage_leg_offset_sweep.py:199–555`; `FREQUENCY_64_HZ` at `test_lossy_sphere_fullwave.py:94`; `GATED_WIDTH_FRACTION` = 0.5 at `test_port_lumped_narrowed_sheet.py:132`.
  - It returns `mesh`, `cell_tags`, `facet_tags`, `problem`, `specs`, `port_defs`, `s`, `z`, `sheets`, `cells` (`:511–555`). The Study is compared against those.

  - **Sheets:** `_sheet_areas` (`:228–238`, via src `_interface_facet_tags`, `io/mesh.py:290`), `_sheet_azimuth_deg` (`:209`), `_projected_extents` (`:186`), `_port_frame` / `_narrowed_radial` (`leg_offset_sweep.py:151–196`), `_sheet_facet_count` (`tests/mesh/test_two_torus_port_sheet.py`) and `_facet_group_area` (`tests/mesh/test_two_torus_port_facets.py`).
  - **Problem:** `leg_offset_sweep.py:357–371`. Background `HomogeneousMaterial(0, 1, 1)`; `material_map` {`CONDUCTOR_CELL_TAG` 1: σ 800, `PHANTOM_CELL_TAG` 3: `SALINE_SIGMA` 0.5 / `SALINE_EPSILON_R` 78}; `"pec_zero_tangential_a"`.

  - Constants: `test_birdcage_port_tags.py:22–32`, `test_birdcage_leg_gaps.py:66`, `test_birdcage_port_sheet_prerequisite.py:52`, `test_birdcage_port_sheets.py:70–77`, `test_port_birdcage_lumped_column.py:100–101`, `test_port_gap_voltage_impedance.py:235`, `test_lossy_sphere_fullwave.py:92–93`, `test_port_package_sparameters.py:140`.

- **The phantom slot.** `GelledSalinePhantomMaterial(SALINE_SIGMA, SALINE_EPSILON_R, FREQUENCY_64_HZ)` goes in as `phantom_material` with the default `phantom_tag` 3, which equals `PHANTOM_CELL_TAG`. The conductor stays in `material_map`.
  - `build_material_fields` writes `float(phantom_material.sigma)` (`core/time_harmonic.py:285–286`) exactly as `material_map` writes `float(tagged_material.sigma)` (`:254–255`). The DG0 arrays are therefore bitwise equal by construction, and (U) checks it.
  - A tag in both maps raises (`:262–265`). The material frequency must equal the problem's (`:462–465`). The phantom-cell check is reduced across ranks (`:277`), so an empty rank at `-n 8` is safe.
- **Touchstone.**
  - `export_touchstone` (`src/fem_em_solver/ports/touchstone.py:111`) writes `.12e` pairs (`:75`) and the frequency at `.9e` (`:84`), row-major for N ≥ 3 (`:88–94`).
  - It writes a `generated_utc` line (`:177`), **so file bytes are never reproducible: compare numbers, never bytes.**
  - It refuses placeholder sweeps (`:141–149`; the flag is set at `ports/sparameters.py:556`).
  - The file is read by `load_touchstone` (`:204`) and by the pinned `skrf` (`tests/environment/test_scikit_rf_version.py`).
- **`PORT-11`'s gates** are as the exact-strip module imports them (`test_g`, `:684–714`):
  - (i) ‖S − Sᵀ‖/‖S‖ ≤ `RECIPROCITY_BAND` 1e-3 (`test_port_lumped_sheet_sweep.py:86`);
  - (ii) σ_max(S) and every column power ≤ 1 + `PASSIVITY_SIGMA_TOLERANCE` 1e-9 (`test_port_birdcage_four_port.py:129`);
  - (iii′) every Z-class spread ≤ `ADJACENT_SPREAD_BAND` 0.005 (`test_port_birdcage_lumped_column.py:121`), and pooled / worst ≥ `POOLED_SEPARATION_FLOOR` 10 (`test_port_birdcage_four_port.py:173`).
  - Helpers: `_circulant_classes` and `_class_spread` (`test_port_birdcage_four_port.py:176`).
  - Expected at 64 MHz, degree 2, `-n 8`: 2.662e-15, σ_max 0.999819887372, spreads 0.0333 / 0.0338 / 0.0441 %, separation 957.45× (`…item192-W3-p2.log:2264`).
  - Expected at degree 1, `-n 2`: 4.509e-16, 0.999760093664, 0.0747 / 0.0691 / 0.0897 %, 451.71× (`…item225-W0-p1-unset.log:2160`).

**Anchors (asserted).**
- **(K) Constants and refusals, structural (no solve).**
  - Every field of `FourPortBirdcageGeometry.f_small()` `==` the imported test constant it restates.
  - A κ keyword raises `TypeError`, and a ragged (`None`) strip fraction raises `ValueError`.
  - `Study(...).degree == 2` without the argument. Every built spec has `width_correction_kappa is None`.
  - All exact.
- **(F) The SPEC rectangle by CAD identity, on the Study's own mesh.** This is the exact-strip module's (F), with the same imports:
  - cells / `ANS4_EXACT_STRIP_CELL_RECORD` (117 206, `test_ops61_exact_strip_ports.py:64`) within `CELL_COUNT_BAND` (`test_birdcage_port_sheet_prerequisite.py:57`);
  - per port, area / w / h / centre radius against `SPEC_*` (`test_ans4_exact_strip_records.py:131–133`) and `RING_RADIUS` within `STRIP_BAND` 1e-9 (`test_ops61_exact_strip_ports.py:58`);
  - full-sheet area ratio within `SHEET_AREA_BAND` (`test_birdcage_leg_offset.py:104`).
- **(U) The Study builds the direct call's problem.**
  - Global cells: Study == direct, exactly, in one process.
  - Per port: tag, global facet count `==`; `h`, `w`, area, azimuth within `IDENTITY_BAND` 1e-12 (`test_birdcage_leg_offset.py:101`, the same-code-path reassociation band).
  - Spec and port-definition fields `==`.
  - DG0 σ / εᵣ / μᵣ built both ways **on the Study's mesh** must read max |Δ| == 0.0 on owned dofs, with `_dg0_owned_diff` (`tests/materials/test_phantom_material_model.py`, as `WF-15` imports it).
  - `sweep.is_placeholder is False`.
- **(R) S equals the record at the leg's degree and width.** The comparator is the exact-strip module's own (`:734`): ‖S_study − S_rec‖_F / ‖S_rec‖_F ≤ `LEG_D0_REPRODUCTION_BAND`.
  - **(R1)** Degree 1, `-n 2`, against `ANS4_EXACT_STRIP_S_P1_RECORDS["64"]`. Backed: 9.557e-11 twice (above). Predicted ≈ 9.6e-11.
  - **(R2)** Degree 2 by default, `-n 8`, against `ANS4_EXACT_STRIP_S_P2_X1["64"]`. Backed by reasoning: ≤ 5e-10 rounding + 8.912e-13 spread. Predicted 1e-11 … 3e-10.
  - **This is the first assertion ever made against `P2_X1`.** The review should note that a printed comparand becomes an asserted one here, on an imported band.
- **(T) The Touchstone file.**
  - **(T1)** Loaded by `load_touchstone` and by `skrf.Network`, the matrix equals the in-memory S at max |ΔS| ≤ `ANCHOR_TOL` 1e-12 (`tests/io/test_touchstone_skrf_crosscheck.py:51`). The `.12e` print bounds this at ≈ 5e-13, by construction.
  - Also in (T1): frequency within `FREQ_ATOL_HZ` (`:52`) of 64 MHz, `nports` 4, port order P1–P4, z0 = 50.
  - **(T2)** The loaded matrix against the leg's record ≤ `LEG_D0_REPRODUCTION_BAND`. This is the row's "Touchstone file equal to the records at 1e-9".
- **(G) `PORT-11`'s gates (i), (ii), (iii′) on the Study's S and Z**, imported as listed above. They are evaluated on the matrices the Study returns, not on the direct rung.
- **(I) The Study equals the direct call end to end, same process.** ‖S_study − S_direct‖ / ‖S_direct‖ ≤ `LEG_D0_REPRODUCTION_BAND`, with the reading printed at `.3e`.
  - Not bitwise, because two MUMPS solves are not bitwise even in one process: `MAT-5` item 237's (R0c) read 2.747e-12 (`20261005T050759Z_MAT-5-step3-W1-alpha0.log:1874–1882`).
  - Backed by the (X) spreads above (≤ 8.912e-13). Predicted ≤ 1e-12.

**Negative controls (rule (e)).**
- **(c1) Cross-degree record swap, asserted.** The (R) comparator, applied to the Study's S against the *other* degree's record, must exceed `LEG_D0_REPRODUCTION_BAND`.
  - Backing: `…item192-W3-p2.log:2223`. The p2-vs-p1 exact 64 MHz class means move 6.67 / 2.54 / 4.52 %, the same two records on the same fixture.
  - The norm-relative distance between the two registered records, by hand arithmetic from `:170–197` and `:262–289`, is ≈ 4.9 %, ≈ 4.9e7× the band. Print the in-test value; it must reproduce ≈ 0.049 to two digits, otherwise the comparator is mis-wired: stop.
- **(c2) Port transposition, asserted on (T2)'s comparator.** Swap P1 ↔ P2 (rows and columns) of the loaded Touchstone matrix and compare it against the record. It must exceed `LEG_D0_REPRODUCTION_BAND`.
  - The swap carries the adjacent class into opposite slots, eight entries off by |adj − opp|. By hand arithmetic on the records it reads ≈ 0.254 (`P2_X1["64"]`) or ≈ 0.249 (`P1["64"]`), ≈ 2.5e8× the band.
  - Backing: the registered record itself. The control is a deterministic function of it, computed in-test and printed before it is asserted. The review may relabel it as predicted.
  - **Why not `PORT-7`'s transpose control:** S here is reciprocal to ≤ 2.7e-15, so S against Sᵀ is blind (`test_touchstone_skrf_crosscheck.py:21–22` says so for reciprocal records). A cyclic port rotation is also near-blind under C4 (it moves S by about the class spreads, ≈ 4e-4). Both are printed, not asserted.
- **(c3) 1-ULP σ through the Study's phantom slot, asserted by construction.** Use `GelledSalinePhantomMaterial(np.nextafter(0.5, 1), 78, f)`. (U)'s DG0 compare must read exactly 2⁻⁵³ = 1.1102230246251565e-16 on > 0 owned dofs (globally reduced). There is no solve.
  - Backing: the assignment copies the scalar (`core/time_harmonic.py:285`), the ulp of 0.5 is 2⁻⁵³, and `MAT-1`'s (c1) measured that exact value on the same code line (`20260930T003229Z_MAT-1-reanchor-W1.log:491`; the sphere fixture, so the mechanism is shared and the fixture is not).
  - Ceiling: this is the smallest nonzero reading, so it shows that (U)'s `== 0.0` is at full resolution.

**Traps already paid for.**
- **No solve output is compared bitwise.** `MAT-1` item 81 went red at 1e-12 (known-issues.md:81), and `MAT-5` item 237 went red at 2.747e-12 in one process. Bitwise belongs upstream of the solve only: (K), (U), (c3).

- **`run_n_port_sparameter_sweep` and `TimeHarmonicSolver` default to degree 1** (`leg_offset_sweep.py:243–249`). (K) and the solved space's degree catch a fall-through.
- **Touchstone under MPI:** write on rank 0 only, `Barrier`, then read. `pytest`'s `tmp_path` differs per rank (`test_touchstone_skrf_crosscheck.py:60`, `_rank_dir`), so use a gitignored `logs/wf16/` path.
  - Compare numbers, not bytes (the `generated_utc` line). Do not write the CSV companion.
- **The transpose control is blind on a reciprocal S** (see (c2)). Do not reuse `PORT-7`'s.
- **Exact rectangles, κ off** (CLAUDE.md binding ruling 2026-10-02). The Study offers no ragged sheet and no κ.
  - Native CAD-port facet tags (`GEO-37`) are **not** used. `dS` over a native tag is unexercised (§10 U6), and the record's tags come from the midpoint filter on CAD strip edges.
- **The phantom frequency must equal the problem's** (`core/time_harmonic.py:462–465`). Pass `FREQUENCY_64_HZ`, not a literal that could differ.
- **Rank-safety.**
  - Sheet extents are reduced elementwise with `allgather` (`leg_offset.py:190–194`), never `allreduce` on arrays.
  - Facet counts and areas are reduced. `cell_tags.values` is rank-local. DG0 compares use the owned slice.
  - S and Z come back replicated from the sweep.
- **The `src/` package never imports `tests/`.** W4 catches it.
- **`WF-14` (N):** node IDs only. The snapshot is sorted. W3 goes red by design if either edit is missing.
- **Orphan `.c`:** sweep only when `pgrep -c python3` reads 0, and never `pgrep -f "python3 -m pytest"` (`bash_guard` denies it). After any kill, run `pgrep -c python3` first.
- **Status 124 is a finding, not a retry.** Never lengthen a timeout and never change `-n`: the widths are the records'.
- **Rule (i):** no edit to the new module, `study.py`, the private helpers, the registry or the snapshot after their last green window.
- **Write ≈, never `~`, in tracked Markdown.** Quote no AED number anywhere. This item touches no AED file.

This is a finding for the review / `record-reconciler` under the (1*) licence. The band never moves in-slot.

- **(T1) red with (R) green:** a writer or reader layout defect on a 4×4. Stop; it is `PORT-7`'s module's business.
- **(G) red:** `PORT-11`'s identities fail through the Study but not through the direct call. This is a real finding. Stop; `WF-16` stays ⬜.
- **(c1), (c2) or (c3) at or under its floor:** the comparator is blind. Stop.

- **W3 red:** a registry or snapshot omission. Fix it (that is in-letter) and re-run W3.
- **W4 red:** the export or the private module broke the installed package. Revert (3); BLOCKED.
- **The box-speed gate fails, or W2 is killed at 590 s:** BLOCKED without a result. The review re-poses.

### §9 item 259 — 2026-10-05 (chain U9, daily provisional)

*Drafted by a general-purpose agent for the 2026-10-05 18:00 daily review; the review reviews it.* Licence: §9 rule (6) (PROJECT_PLAN.md:3707–3717). This is a provisional next step of live chain U, toward the chain's own validation target ("each step reproduces a gated result within its unmoved band", :4649–4651) and Phase 9 subgoal (9a)'s "solve (frequency / sweep …) and results (S / Z / Touchstone …)". It is step 1's own hypothesis for next (attempts.md:2535). No band, tolerance, Done-when or validation target moves. Every band is imported by name; none is set here. Depends on nothing (item 244 is on `main`).

**`WF-16` step 2: the frequency sweep through the `Study`.** One `Study(FourPortBirdcageGeometry.f_small(), phantom, LumpedPortDrive(), frequency_hz=FREQUENCY_64_HZ[, degree=1])` is constructed once. It is solved at 10, 64 and 128 MHz on **one mesh** and returns one `PortStudyResult` per frequency plus **one** multi-frequency `.s4p`. Each frequency's S, in memory and as parsed back from the file, equals item 192's registered exact-rectangle record at that frequency within `LEG_D0_REPRODUCTION_BAND`, at the record's own degree and width. `PORT-11`'s gates are imported per frequency.
- **Executor:** implementer. Additive `src/` change in `study.py` only, one new test module, and one registry edit. No existing gate module, helper, record, test or `src/` line is edited.
- **Tier:** W0 standard. W1 heavy by ceiling (`-n 2`, container 400 s). W2 heavy (`-n 8`, 590 s). W3 / W4 standard. Complex build for W0–W2.
- **Class:** chain step (solve). Measured median ≈ 9 min (the 18:00 review's restock figure). Package steps have only one measurement so far (step 1, ≈ 10 min of windows).
- **Cost:** ≈ 50 predicted min. That is 15 fixed + ≈ 18 code + windows ≈ 2.0 + 3.3 + 0.2 + 5.1–6.3 + 0.1 + 0.6 ≈ 12 min + ≈ 5 spare. **If W1 is not green by minute 35, park** (rule (d)).

**API decision (the executor may rename; the shape is fixed by the constraints).**
- (S1) **A new method, `Study.solve_sweep(frequencies_hz, comm=MPI.COMM_WORLD)`.** It returns a new `PortStudySweepResult`. Alternatives rejected:
  - Making `frequency_hz` accept a sequence edits an existing `__init__` line (`self.frequency_hz = float(frequency_hz)`) and breaks `Study.omega` / `epsilon_c` for the sphere family.
  - A constructor `frequencies_hz=` keyword forces `frequency_hz` to become optional, another signature edit.
  - A sweep list on `LumpedPortDrive` splits frequency between two objects.
  - The method mirrors the HFSS "setup + frequency sweep" split: `frequency_hz` stays the Study's solution frequency for `solve()`, and the sweep list is the sweep. Nothing in `Study.__init__`, `Study.solve`, `_study_solve_ports`, `FourPortBirdcageGeometry`, `LumpedPortDrive` or `PortStudyResult` changes.
- (S2) **What `solve_sweep` does.**
  - Validation: refuse a non-port Study (`TypeError`, as `mean_sar` does for the converse). Refuse an empty, non-finite, non-positive, or not strictly increasing list (`ValueError`, not a silent sort, because `export_touchstone` needs non-decreasing frequencies, `ports/touchstone.py:65`, and result order must equal input order).
  - `geometry.build(comm)` is called **once**. The specs and port definitions are built **once** with `LumpedPortDrive.specs_and_definitions`.
  - Then, per frequency `f`:
    - a fresh `TimeHarmonicProblem` with the same lines as `_study_solve_ports` (`study.py:434–447`), with `frequency_hz=f` and `phantom_material=dataclasses.replace(self.material, frequency_hz=f)`;
    - `run_n_port_sparameter_sweep(..., degree=self.degree)`, called exactly as `study.py:451–457` calls it;
    - one `PortStudyResult`.
  - **The problem lines are copied, not factored out of `_study_solve_ports`.** A shared helper would edit the scalar path and oblige step 1's degree-2 W2 (268 s) to re-run, which this slot cannot hold.
  - `self.frequency_hz`, `self.material` and `self.result` are left untouched. The result is also stored as `self.sweep_result`.
  - **Constant-property gel across the sweep:** σ, εᵣ and μᵣ are copied, and only the frequency tag changes. This is what item 192's records solved: `material_map` `HomogeneousMaterial(0.5, 78)` at every frequency (`leg_offset_sweep.py:357–371`). The docstring says there is no dispersion model.
- (S3) **`PortStudySweepResult`** (dataclass):
  - fields: `frequencies_hz` (tuple), `results` (list of `PortStudyResult`, all sharing one `mesh`), `mesh_time`, `sweep_times`, `comm`;
  - property `s_matrices` (an `(F, 4, 4)` complex128 array);
  - method `.write_touchstone(path)`. It calls `export_touchstone([r.sweep for r in results], results[0].port_definitions, output_path=path, write_csv_companion=False)` **on rank 0 only**, then `Barrier`, and returns the path on every rank (the `PortStudyResult.write_touchstone` pattern, `study.py:405–421`). `export_touchstone` already writes N sweeps row-major per block (`:183–186`) and `load_touchstone` already reads them, so no `ports/` edit is needed.
  - **Not exported**, as `PortStudyResult` was not. `__init__.py` and `public_surface.txt` are therefore unchanged. If the executor finds that a new top-level name is needed, it follows item 244's Code (4) verbatim.
- (S4) **Allowed diff:** `git diff src/fem_em_solver/study.py` shows **insertions only**, plus at most the module `__all__` line. W1 runs step 1's module unedited in the same process to show that the scalar path is unchanged.

**Negative controls (rule (e)).**
- **(c4) Frequency confusion matrix, asserted.** Compare every loaded block `i` against every record key `j` with the (T2′) comparator. The diagonal is ≤ band (that is (T2′)); **every off-diagonal entry must exceed `LEG_D0_REPRODUCTION_BAND`**.
  - Hand arithmetic on the registered records (pure Python from `:141–318`), ‖rec_i − rec_j‖/‖rec_j‖:

    | rows i → columns j | `P1` | `P2_X1` |
    |---|---|---|
    | 10 → 64 / 128 | 0.8103 / 1.2367 | 0.8395 / 1.2592 |
    | 64 → 10 / 128 | 0.8120 / 0.5524 | 0.8515 / 0.5594 |
    | 128 → 10 / 64 | 1.2763 / 0.5689 | 1.3138 / 0.5755 |

  - Minimum 0.5524, ≈ 5.5e8× the band. The in-test matrix is printed first and must reproduce these to two digits; otherwise the comparator is mis-wired: stop.
  - This is the control a frequency-blind sweep (every point solved at `self.frequency_hz`) fails, and it is also what (R′) at 10 / 128 MHz would read in that case.
- **(c5) Planted block swap in the parsed file, asserted.** Swap the loaded 10 MHz and 128 MHz blocks, keeping the frequency labels, and re-run (T2′):

  - the 64 MHz entry stays ≤ band.

- **(c6) Resolution of the band, asserted by construction.** Add δ = 3·`LEG_D0_REPRODUCTION_BAND`·‖rec["64"]‖_F to entry (0, 1) of the loaded 64 MHz block. (T2′)'s comparator must then read > band.
  - Backing: the triangle inequality, ≥ 3e-9 − (T2′ reading) > 2e-9.

- **Executor:** implementer. Additive `src/` (one new public class, one new private solve function, one result subclass) plus **one 3-line insertion inside an existing refusal branch** (below); one new test module; registry, surface snapshot and API pages.

Predicted W1 140–300 s (below). **Class:** chain step (solve). **Cost:** ≈ 51 predicted min (15 fixed + ≈ 25 code + W0 0.3 + W1 ≈ 3–5 + RG1 2.6 + Wgen / Wapi / Wdoc 0.3 + W13 0.6 + ≈ 4 spare). **If W1 has not started by minute 38, park** (rule (d)).

**API (names are the weekly's; the shape is the drafter's — the review may amend, the executor adapts names only and discloses).**
- **`CapacitorSheets(c_f, esr_ohm=0.0, *, on, drive=LumpedPortDrive())`**, a frozen dataclass in `study.py`, accepted in the `Study`'s **excitation** slot. Every sheet whose port id is in `on` carries the series-RC termination `Z = series_rlc_impedance(f, r_ohm=esr_ohm, l_h=0.0, c_f=c_f)` (`src/fem_em_solver/ports/lumped.py:138`); every other sheet is a lumped port with `drive`'s z0 / impedance / voltage, driven in turn. Method `impedance_ohm(frequency_hz)`. `__post_init__` refuses (`ValueError`) non-finite or non-positive `c_f`, negative or non-finite `esr_ohm`, an empty or duplicated `on`, and a non-`LumpedPortDrive` `drive`.
- **The dispatch hook — inside the existing refusal branch, so no pre-existing route executes a new statement.** In `_study_solve_ports` (`study.py:606–607`) the branch `if not isinstance(self.excitation, LumpedPortDrive): raise TypeError(...)` gains, *before* its `raise`: `hook = getattr(self.excitation, "_solve_port_study", None)`; `if hook is not None: return hook(self, comm)`. A `LumpedPortDrive` never enters the branch, so every existing route (four-port, sweep-free copper, F-human, YAML, the tutorials, the bundle) executes exactly its pre-change statements; the auditor can check this on the diff. U36 (item 382) reuses the same hook for its drive, so it needs no further edit to this function.
- **`CapacitorSheets._solve_port_study(study, comm)`** delegates to a new private `_study_solve_capacitor_sheets(study, comm)`:
  1. Refuse, **before any mesh**: an `FHumanBirdcageGeometry` (`TypeError`, U36's territory); an `on` id outside `P1 … P{leg_count}` (`ValueError`); an `on` that leaves no port (`ValueError`).
  2. Build the geometry and the problem with the **same lines** as `_study_solve_ports` (`study.py:611–631`: `geom.build`, `_conductor_setup`, the `material_map` / `as_hole` branch, `phantom_material`), copied, not factored out (a shared helper would edit the scalar path — step 2's rejected alternative, item 259 (S1)).

  4. Drive each **kept** port (ascending port order, the order `PORT-24`'s `_terminated_three_port` uses, `tests/validation/test_port24_series_rc_sheet.py:103–121`) with `run_lumped_sheet_port_case(problem, port_defs` **(all four)**`, specs′, facet_tags=…, driven_port_id=…, degree=study.degree, extra_bilinear_terms=cond_terms, solver=shared)`: the first drive factorises, the rest reuse the factor (the sweep's own reuse lines, `ports/sparameters.py:434–460`; legal because every drive shares `specs′`, `PORT-19`'s premise). `S_kj = b_k/a_j` by `_power_waves(V, I, z0)` (`ports/sparameters.py:72`), z0 the kept ports' common `z0_ohm`.
  5. Return a new **`CapacitorStudyResult(PortStudyResult)`** (not exported, as `PortStudyResult` is not): `s_matrix` K×K over the kept ports; `port_definitions` = the kept ones; new fields `all_port_definitions`, `capacitor_port_ids`, `capacitor_impedance_ohm`; `specs` = `specs′` (all four); `sweep = None`, `z_matrix = None`; `write_touchstone` raises `NotImplementedError` (a reduced-network Touchstone is out of scope).

E_s = E₁₇ + w_s,₂₁·E₂₁ on one dof array. `s_node` by the copied `_current_route_s`, `y_in` per sense by the copied `_active_y_in`.

**What the code says (read by this drafter on `fed0e27c`, not run).** The brief's premise is stated precisely here because it differs from the shorter form in which it reached the review.
- `_study_solve_ports` (`study.py:609–676`) calls `cond_kwargs, cond_terms = _conductor_setup(self, built, comm)` at `:625`, maps the conductor tag only on the solid route (`material_map=({} if geom.as_hole else {…})`, `:632–636`), passes `**cond_kwargs` to `TimeHarmonicProblem` (`:640`: `facet_tags=` the outer-box meshtags, `pec_facet_tags=(OUTER_BOX_TAG,)`), passes `extra_bilinear_terms=cond_terms` to the sweep only when set (`:651`), and stores `conductor_terms` / `conductor_facet_tags` on the result (`:673–674`).
- `_study_solve_port_fields` (`:1592–1660`) does **none** of that: no `_conductor_setup`, an unconditional `material_map={geom.conductor_tag: …}` (`:1619–1623`), no conductor keywords, no `extra_bilinear_terms`, no `conductor_*` fields on its result.

- **So the defect has two faces, and neither is "silently Leontovich-less fields on the hole route":**
  - **(F1) Hole route (`as_hole=True`, conductor `LeontovichWall()` *or* `None`): `Study.sar` / `b1_plus` / `b1_plus_at` raise** `ValueError: material_map references tags that do not exist in problem.cell_tags: [1]`, because the hole mesh has no tag-1 cells. Loud, not silent: the copper `Study`'s SAR and `|B₁⁺|` outputs have simply never run.
  - **(F2) Solid route + `LeontovichWall()` (`as_hole=False`): `Study.sar` / `b1_plus` silently return the σ = 800 S/m solid-conductor fields**, while `Study.solve()` on the same Study refuses with `_conductor_setup`'s `ValueError`. **The fix (one function, copied lines, no shared helper).** In `_study_solve_port_fields`, between `msh = built["mesh"]` and the `TimeHarmonicProblem(...)` call, and in the same order as `_study_solve_ports`:

5. `conductor_terms=cond_terms, conductor_facet_tags=built["mesh_facet_tags"] if cond_terms else None` on the `PortStudyResult` (`:673–674`; existing fields, no new field).

**API (additive, insertions only in `src/fem_em_solver/study.py`).**
- `Study.tune(k: int = 1, *, comm=MPI.COMM_WORLD)` returns a non-exported `TuneResult` (`k`, `frequency_hz`, `z_k` (4 complex), `c_mode_farad`, `off_diagonal_residual`).
- It reuses `self.result` and solves once if that is absent, as `mean_sar` does.
- It is defined on a port Study with `LumpedPortDrive` only. Every other Study raises `TypeError` before meshing: the sphere, the F-human / `TwoPortQuadratureDrive` Study and `CapacitorSheets`.
- **The arithmetic:**
  - Z = `s_to_z(S, z0)` (`src/fem_em_solver/ports/circuit.py:165`).
  - F is the unitary 4-point DFT, as in `TH-17`'s `_four_port_dft` (`tests/validation/test_th17_birdcage_eigenmodes.py:786–788`), copied with a provenance comment; `src/` never imports `tests/`.
  - z_k = diag(F·Z·Fᴴ).
  - `k` must be 1 or 2. Im z_k ≤ 0 raises `ValueError`; k = 0 always does, since z₀ = 2.01 − 1164.9j Ω on the PEC means.
- **ESR.** Under the 2026-10-06 ruling (i), a uniform r shifts Re z_k only, so `C_mode` does not depend on r. The step therefore takes no `esr_ohm` argument; the docstring says this and labels the result lossless.

**Scope.** Covered: k = 1 `C_mode` through the API on the exact-rectangle copper (Leontovich, σ = 5.8e7 S/m) hole F-small (80 798 cells, κ off), degree 2, 64 MHz, `-n 8`, lossless capacitor, reproducing `ANS-7` T21's same-fixture copper reading. **Not claimed:**
- any h-statement: one mesh, and the conductor grading moved the self class 1.92 % (§2);

**Precondition check on `fdd2d03f` — what the API can and cannot build (drafter's reading of the code; nothing run).**
- **The Study can put capacitor sheets on the PEC hole with no new `src/` code.** `CapacitorSheets(c_f, esr_ohm=0.0, on=…, drive=LumpedPortDrive())` (`src/fem_em_solver/study.py:690–731`) dispatches to `_study_solve_capacitor_sheets` (`:746–`), which builds the same geometry and problem lines as the four-port route, including the hole route's empty `material_map` (`:775–779`). With `conductor=None`, `_conductor_setup` returns `({}, None)` (`:1972–1976`): natural PEC on `as_hole=True`, exactly item 429's fixture. The capacitor sheets **are the four leg-gap port sheets** (P1–P4) of `FourPortBirdcageGeometry` — F-small has no separate ring-gap sheets — which is where `TH-17`'s / `Study.tune`'s `C_mode` lives (one capacitor per gap). Nothing is missing.
- **But no in-model 4×4 at `C_mode` exists, by construction of the API.** `CapacitorSheets` requires `on` non-empty and refuses an `on` that leaves no port to drive (`study.py:713–719`, `:764–765`); `Study.solve` returns the reduced S over the **kept** ports (`:789–790`), so the largest in-model "tuned" matrix is 3×3 (one capacitor) and the PORT-22 construction is 1×1 (P1 kept, P2–P4 at C). The weekly's "the in-model 4×4 at `C_mode`" is therefore read as **the in-model reduced networks at `C_mode`** — Route A (`on=("P2","P3","P4")`, `PORT-22`'s own construction, `tests/validation/test_port22_driven_sweep_resonance.py:15–17`) and the four per-sheet 3×3s (`PORT-24` / item 373's construction) — each against `reduce_terminated_ports(S₀, z0, …)` (`src/fem_em_solver/ports/circuit.py:81`) on the same Study's untuned 4×4. That is `PORT-22` (i)'s circuit = field identity on this fixture, as the weekly named it.
- **The "Im z₁ of the tuned matrix" clause is true by construction (rule (h) flag, for the review).** The only 4×4 that carries a tuned z₁ is the circuit-side Z_t = Z(S₀) + z_cap·I₄, and diag(F·Z_t·Fᴴ)[1] = z₁ + 1/(jωC_mode) = Re z₁ + j(Im z₁ − Im z₁) — `TH-17`'s own `_loop_z(z₁, f, C)` (`tests/validation/test_th17_birdcage_eigenmodes.py:854–856`, "zero at a resonance of the four-capacitor structure") evaluated at the C that `tune` defined from that same z₁ (`study.py:1055`). Its imaginary part is rounding (predicted ≤ 1e-13 × \|z₁\|). The field-side alternative does not separate the modes: Route A's Z_in(P1) + z_cap is ≈ 0.0500 − 5.1e-5j Ω at `C_mode` **and** ≈ 0.0120 + 5.8e-6j Ω at the k = 2 capacitance (drafter's unlogged circulant arithmetic on item 429's printed z_k, `20261014T004318Z_WF-16-s6-W1.log:945`), because P1 couples to modes 1, 2 and 3 alike — so a field-side "Im ≈ 0" reading would let the k = 2 control pass, not miss. **Pose:** the clause is asserted as the weekly wrote it, on the circuit-side Z_t, and **labelled a by-construction identity (a record, rule (h)) in the docstring, the node name and the journal**; the field-side Route-A reading is printed beside it for both capacitances. The review may instead demote it to printed only.
- **The circuit = field identity holds at any C** (it is a linear-network identity; item 373 measured it at C_F with r = 0.2 and 0). It is therefore not a tuning statement by itself; the k = 2 control below shows its *comparator* reads C, and the printed S_A(C_k2) reading shows the identity is C-agnostic.

### Review ruling 2026-10-15 18:00 (item 472, the (K0) re-band)

**Ratified.** (K0) is the W0 check that the ring read does not depend on port row order. Unshuffled and shuffled copies measured 1 ulp apart (1.1813757652308145e-10 against …148e-10, `20261015T213212Z_WF-16-s12-W0.log:63`). `s_to_z` inverts the two row orders in different floating-point sequences. Both shuffled reads equal the closed form and `ring_mode_rows` at 0.000e+00 (`:52`). The brief's "bit for bit" was a drafting slip: a reordered linear solve has no such identity. The project's band for exact identities under rounding is `EXACT_IDENTITY_RTOL`. The executor re-registered the clause on the same quantity and disclosed it in the commit, §9, this file and the code comment. That is rule (f)'s path, and the review now ratifies it. No gate rests on the bitwise clause: (R0), (I), (a) and (c1) were each green at their pre-registered bands. **Standing note for drafters:** do not pre-register "bit for bit" on a quantity computed through a dense inverse or solve whose row order differs between the two sides. Use `EXACT_IDENTITY_RTOL`.

### §9 item 722 — 2026-11-01 (§10 U108, chain U, 9a — `WF-16` step 17: `Study.load_result` on a multi-frequency Touchstone file; chain step (solve), standard, complex)

*Enumerated by the 10-31 weekly: "the claim U94 did not make". Depends on nothing. Family U108, attempt 1 of 4.*

**One execution detail, returned to the 11-02 weekly.** This step lifts a refusal that a test and two pages pin: `test_wf16_study_load_result.py:91–94` asserts `ValueError` "exactly one frequency" on a two-frequency file, and `docs/guide/tuning_a_saved_result.md:52` and `tuning_a_saved_human_scale_result.md:53` say the same. The weekly's `tune(at_hz=…)` cannot exist unless the load succeeds. **So:** a multi-frequency file now loads on a four-port `Study`, and the refusal moves one call later: `tune` without `at_hz` on a multi-frequency result raises `ValueError` naming `at_hz`. The single-frequency path is unchanged. The test's one tuple entry moves to that new refusal, and the two sentences are corrected. Disclosed in the outcome. No band moves.

**Route (`src/fem_em_solver/study.py`, additive beyond that).**
- `LoadedPortResult` (`:1264`) gains `frequencies_hz: tuple = ()` and `s_matrices: Any = None`, both defaulted, so a single-frequency result is unchanged field by field.
- `_study_load_result` (`:1288`): `freqs.size == 1` runs today's path exactly. `freqs.size > 1`, **on a `FourPortBirdcageGeometry` only** (32- and 64-port multi-frequency files stay refused with today's message, because nothing has gated them): the Study's `frequency_hz` must be inside the span; store the stack. `s_matrix` holds the column nearest the Study's frequency, so a consumer that reads it sees one matrix.
- `_study_tune` (`:1149`) gains keyword `at_hz=None`. Given on a multi-frequency loaded result: select the nearest frequency, refuse `at_hz` outside [min, max] with `ValueError` before any arithmetic, and use `2π f_selected`, **not `self.omega`**, in `c = 1/(ω Im z_k)`. Given on any other result: `TypeError`. `TuneResult.frequency_hz` reports the selected frequency.
- `_study_tune_ring`: `at_hz` is not forwarded; an explicit value raises `TypeError` on the ring route, as `n_slots` does on the four-port route.

**New module `tests/package/test_wf16_load_result_sweep.py`.** The file: **`logs/wf16/wf16_sweep_f_small_p1_n2.s4p`**, U71's sweep output (present today; gitignored; **absent ⇒ fail, never re-solve**), three frequencies. Its coding twin writes a synthetic three-frequency file through `_write` (`test_wf16_study_load_result.py`) and needs no log.

**Anchors.**
- (Sel) `tune(k=1, at_hz=f)` selects the file's nearest frequency, asserted equal to the record's, on an exact frequency and on a midpoint-side value;
- (C=) `C_mode` at the selected frequency equals the single-frequency tune of that column, written to a one-frequency file in `tmp_path`, at `REDUCTION_BAND`;
- (ω) the selected ω is used: at 10 MHz, C equals 1/(2π·10⁷·Im z_k) on the stored column, ≤ `REDUCTION_BAND`;
- (S0) 0 solves, with `solve`, `build` and `gmsh.initialize` patched to raise;
- (Ref) `at_hz` outside the span is refused; `tune()` with no `at_hz` on a multi-frequency result is refused; a multi-frequency `.s32p` is still refused.

### §9 item 780 — 2026-11-03 (§10 U115, chain U — 9a, a public field route on a sweep; `WF-16` step 18)

**Executes** §10 U115. **The name, picked by this review:** `Study.solve_sweep(frequencies_hz, comm, *, fields_at=None)`, one keyword on the existing method (`study.py:1067–1155`). ### §9 item 993 — 2026-11-15 (§10 U13a, chain U — rule (4), the 11-14 weekly's re-pose; chain step (no solve))

*Enumerated by the 11-14 weekly at `PROJECT_PLAN.md:5020–5039` (the ruling) and `:5257–5260` (the step). Brief drafted 2026-11-15 as execution detail. A record plus CAD identities: no band is set or moved. Family U13a, attempt 1 of 4. **Executor:** the implementer, in-slot (item 985 precedent). The weekly's target binds: "a CadQuery-authored F-small four-port (the 1d authoring script's pattern, four named sheets at the generator's exact-rectangle positions) imported through U12′'s route: (CAD) the four sheets tagged with fragment lineage and each area at `STRIP_BAND` at `-n 1 / 2 / 4`; (Q) a mesh-quality reading (min dihedral angle, max aspect) on the un-optimised mesh printed beside the primitive mesh's; no solve. Validation target: the CAD identities at `STRIP_BAND`."*

**What the step is.** U12′ (item 985, `b8214906`) proved the named-sheet route on a unit box: a CadQuery STEP whose free face `PortSheet` reaches `import_step` by name (`highest_dim_only: false` + `surface_groups`), is embedded by `occ.fragment` and tagged 201 with its area exact. U13a moves that route to the F-small four-port: four CadQuery-named sheets placed where `MeshGenerator.birdcage_port_domain(leg_sheet_strip_fraction=0.5)` puts its exact centre strips (`OPS-61` step 1b), imported, tagged by name, each area at `STRIP_BAND`; and, because the route skips Netgen (known-issues 2026-11-14), the un-optimised mesh's quality is read beside the primitive's. U13b (serial on this) is the solve.

**Facts verified against the tree (this review, by `grep` / reading; unlogged, not a record).**
- **`STRIP_BAND = 1.0e-9`** at `tests/validation/test_ops61_exact_strip_ports.py:58`; U12′ restated it as a literal with that provenance comment (`tests/mesh/test_step_import_cq_port_sheet.py:43`). U13a **imports** it (`from tests.validation.test_ops61_exact_strip_ports import STRIP_BAND`, the `test_birdcage_implant_spheroid_f_human.py:51` precedent), never restates it.
- **U12′'s route:** `src/fem_em_solver/io/step_import.py` config keys `highest_dim_only` and `surface_groups` (name → tag by `getEntityName` + fragment `out_map`, never a geometric selector). With `surface_groups` set the Netgen pass is skipped (`:320–330`, the `if named_surfaces: pass` branch); key absent ⇒ `optimize("Netgen")` unchanged. Config shape: `tests/mesh/data/geo34_step1d_port_sheet.json` (`resolution`, `outer_boundary_tag`, `highest_dim_only`, `volume_groups` with `bbox_contains` selectors, `surface_groups` entries `{name, step_name, role, tag}`). The import's diagnostics carry `diag["surface_groups"][name]` with `tag`, `role`, `step_name`, `cad_area`, `n_triangles`, `route` (test `:91–100`).
- **U12′'s gates to copy:** (P1) owned-facet area vs analytic and vs `cad_area` at `STRIP_BAND` (`:89–102`); (P2) owned count == `n_triangles`, 0 one-sided (`:105–116`); (P3) `dS(tag)` at `STRIP_BAND·A` (`:119–132`); (P4) global tag set + `validate_required_port_tags_exist` (`:135–144`); (n3) drop-one-facet control (`:147–157`); (n-hDO) default read raises naming the sheet (`:160–166`); (n1) absent name raises everywhere (`:169–173`); (n2) without `surface_groups` no port tag (`:176–184`). W1 `20261114T181927Z_GEO-34-U12p-W1.log`: 10 passed per width, **Elapsed 11 s** for `-n 1 / 2 / 4` (`:728–729`; command `:12`, `PYTHONPATH=/workspace/src:/workspace`, `timeout -k 30 60` per width).
- **The authoring pattern:** `tests/mesh/cq_author_geo34_step1d.py` (64 lines) runs under `cq-python`, which **clears `PYTHONPATH`** (`docker/Dockerfile:41`, cited at `:4`), so it cannot import the generator; it restates its constants as literals with provenance, builds each sheet as an explicit 4-corner `cq.Face.makeFromWires(cq.Wire.makePolygon(...))` (never `makePlane`), asserts area at 1e-12 and bbox at 1e-6, and exports a `cq.Assembly` of named products (`:46–54`). The STEP is committed once with a sha256 pin (test `:38`) and a narrow `.gitignore` negation (`b8214906`).
- **The generator's exact-rectangle positions:** `src/fem_em_solver/io/mesh.py:5568–5591`. Each leg's sheet is `addRectangle(port_radius − ½·port_dx, −½·port_dz, 0, port_dx, port_dz)` at azimuth 0 in the xy plane; with `leg_sheet_strip_fraction = f` the centre rectangle `addRectangle(port_radius − ½·f·port_dx, −½·port_dz, 0, f·port_dx, port_dz)` is fragmented out **before** placement, and every piece takes `_place_sheet_in_leg_frame(piece, box_angle)` (`:414–434`: one snapped affine `R_z(angle)·R_x(π/2)`, so at 0 / 90 / 180 / 270° corners stay on exact coordinates). The numbers `port_radius`, `port_dx`, `port_dz` and each `box_angle` are **not** module constants; read them from the built model (`diag["port_box_size_m"]` gives `(dx, dy, dz)`, `test_ops61_exact_strip_ports.py:85`).
- **The comparand:** `OPS-61`'s (A2) — every exact strip N_i = 0.5·dx·dz within `STRIP_BAND` (`test_ops61_exact_strip_ports.py:15–16`, asserted `:193–199`); the full sheet is `SHEET_AREA_M2 = 1.120000000e-04` (`tests/mesh/test_birdcage_phantom_resolution.py:69`), so the strip is 5.6e-05 m² by arithmetic (assert against `0.5·dx·dz` from the model, not this literal).
- **The primitive mesh for (Q):** `_build(np.zeros(LEG_COUNT), leg_sheet_strip_fraction=0.5)` (`tests/mesh/test_birdcage_port_sheets.py:80–90`, called at `test_ops61_exact_strip_ports.py:81–83`); flag-on record 117 206 cells at `-n 1` (`:65`, `ANS4_EXACT_STRIP_CELL_RECORD`). Netgen runs on it (key-absent generator path).
- **A quality helper exists:** `_tet_quality(x)` in `tests/mesh/probe_wf6_sheet_facet_census.py:83–110` returns per-tet radius ratio `3 r_in / R_circ` and min dihedral (degrees) from an `(n, 4, 3)` coordinate array. Import it; "max aspect" = max of `1 / (3 r_in / R_circ)` (state the definition in the print).
- **The OCC writer drops named sheets** (known-issues 2026-10-07), which is why 1a's STEP cannot carry them; **1b's own round trip prints "mesh differs"** (`tests/mesh/test_step_import_four_port.py:26–28`).
- **Known-issues 2026-11-14** (`docs/testing/known-issues.md:895–902`): Netgen "456 illegal tets" then SEGV on the 1d fixture (`20261114T181903Z_GEO-34-U12p-W1.log:111–116`, Status 15, 2 s); cause undiagnosed (`GEO-34` 1e, item 996, is the probe); **Resolves with:** "a measured quality reading on the un-optimised named-sheet mesh with the step's gates green, or an optimiser route that keeps the embedded face. Never by re-enabling the pass on the key-absent path." U13a green is the first half of that clause.

**Pre-registered design choice (the review's; flag if the slot finds otherwise).** "CadQuery-authored F-small four-port" is read as: **the solids come from the generator's own STEP export** (1a's `step_export_path=` on `_build`, `emit_port_sheets=True`, strip flag off; the OCC writer drops the sheets, which is what we want) **re-read inside CadQuery** (`cq.importers.importStep`), and the **four named strip faces are authored in CadQuery** at the generator's positions and added as products `PortSheet1` … `PortSheet4` of one `cq.Assembly`. Authoring the whole birdcage in CadQuery from parameters is `GEO-34` step 1c (independent kernel), not this step. If `importStep` + `Assembly.export` loses the solids' names or merges them, **stop and report** — do not switch to a full CadQuery rebuild in-slot.

**Review note (2026-11-15 18:00 daily).** The drafter's open points are ruled as follows: the W0b′ sha256 env pin replaces the committed `SHA256` literal; (n-split) is accepted as the measurement of the ruling's premise; the strip's ±z edges on the box faces are this item's first reading, with the disposition stated under Negative result. U13b's port reading (`ports/_sheet_geometry.py:93–97` finds a port as a halves interface) goes to the 11-16 weekly with option (b); it does not block this item.

**What changed since 993: the review's ruling, option (a).** Item 993's W1a (`20261115T210648Z_WF-16-U13a-W1-n1.log:102`) read `surface group 'port_1': fragment child 241 of 'PortSheet1' is not embedded in any volume`. The cause is the solids, not the route. With `emit_port_sheets=True`, each port box is two halves, `port_P<i>_lower` / `_upper`. They share the full dx × dz sheet, so the CadQuery f = 0.5 strip lies **on** that interface. After the heal it is a boundary face of two volumes, and `_embed_named_surfaces` accepts only an embedded child (`src/fem_em_solver/io/step_import.py:427–452`). **Ruling (a):** export each port box **whole**. The f = 0.5 strip is then a face embedded inside one volume, which is the topology U12′ proved (`tests/mesh/cq_author_geo34_step1d.py:19`: a 0.8 × 0.6 face at z = 0.47 strictly inside the unit box). The config gains **one port volume group per leg** in place of 1a's verbatim halves. *(Option (b) is a licensed `_embed_named_surfaces` lineage for a named face shared by exactly two volumes; it is Netgen-safe per item 996's arm S. It is a `src/` change and goes back to the weekly; it is not this item.)*

**Facts verified against the tree (this review, by `grep` / reading; unlogged, not a record).**
- **The knob exists: `emit_port_sheets=False` gives whole port boxes, and nothing has to be fused in CadQuery.** In leg-gap mode the port box is built whole and rotated (`src/fem_em_solver/io/mesh.py:5547–5556`). The only thing that splits it is the sheet added under `if emit_port_sheets and leg_gap_length is not None:` (`:5558`). That block is also the only place `sheet_of_ordinal[ordinal]` is set (`:5591`). In the piece policy, a port-box piece whose ordinal is in no sheet map takes `100 + ordinal` whole (`:6145–6146`) and is named `port_P{idx}` with no `_lower` / `_upper` (`:6436–6441`). `expected_groups` then holds `100 + i` only (`:6485–6488`). Gap mode with sheets off is legal: the guard at `:4716` refuses sheets without a gap, not a gap without sheets. `leg_sheet_strip_fraction` needs `emit_port_sheets=True` (`:4626–4628`), so the strip flag stays off, as in 993; the strips come from CadQuery. The STEP is written after the fragment and before meshing (`:6983–6985`), so it carries the whole-box pieces.
- **The call is `_build(False, step_export_path=…)`** (`tests/mesh/test_birdcage_port_sheets.py:80–90`; positional `emit_port_sheets`, forwarded at `:161`). The flag-off build already runs in the tree as GEO-18 step 2's control (`:266`), whose port groups are `PORT_LOWER + i` = 101…104 (`:73`, `:268–270`). The gap is unchanged (`leg_gap_length=LEG_GAP_LENGTH`, `:160`). The box's z-extent is the gap (`sheet_analytic = dx * dz`, "dx*g", `:262`, `:275–280`).

Then print the four placed strip corners as before. **Pre-gate (printed, then compared by the slot):** the flag-off port_radius, dx and dz equal W0a's `:1796` values bit for bit. If they differ, stop. If the flag-off diag lacks `port_layout`, read port_radius from the flag-on build and say so.
2. **Author script.** Keep it as is apart from these changes: the docstring cites W0a′ and the whole-box export; the generator path becomes an argument, so there is no default to the stale 993 path; and it prints the imported solid count. W0b′ runs it twice: whole → `u13a_fsmall_four_port_whole.step`, halves → `u13a_fsmall_four_port_halves.step`. Expected by arithmetic: 30 solids for whole (993's 34 less one per merged pair). That is printed, not gated, **but 34 on the whole file ⇒ stop** (the halves survived).
3. **Config** `tests/mesh/data/u13a_fsmall_four_port.json`: the volume groups as listed above (conductor / air / phantom verbatim, four whole-box groups). The four `surface_groups` are unchanged (`port_<i>` / `PortSheet<i>` / `port` / `230 + i`). Rewrite the description string to say what this config is.
4. **Test** `tests/mesh/test_step_import_u13a_fsmall.py`:
   - `STEP` = `/workspace/logs/u13a_prime/u13a_fsmall_four_port_whole.step` and `STEP_HALVES` = `…_halves.step`.
   - The `SHA256` literal is dropped. `test_custody_and_config` instead asserts that `sha256(STEP)` equals `os.environ["U13A_STEP_SHA256"]`, and a missing variable is an error, not a skip. It also prints bytes and both sha256 values.
   - Each `port_P<i>` group claims exactly one volume (`diag["volumes_by_group"]`, asserted). Its `cad_mass_by_group` is printed beside dx·dy·dz.
   - (C2) becomes (C2′) above.
   - n-hDO's `match` narrows to `"no dim-2 entity named 'PortSheet1'"`. 993's `match="PortSheet1"` also matched the embed error (993 Outcome), so it read nothing.
   - Add (n-split), below.
   - Everything else is unchanged.

**Gates (asserted; no band set or moved).** These run at `-n 1`, `-n 2` and `-n 4`, one width per window:
- (P0) ghosts;
- (C0) one volume per port group;
- (C1) area vs 0.5·dx·dz and vs `cad_area` within `STRIP_BAND`;
- (C2′) owned count == `n_triangles`, every strip facet two cells, both tagged `100 + i`;
- (C3) `dS(230 + i)` within `STRIP_BAND·A`;
- (C4) `step_name` / `route` carry the lineage (`"… (embedded)"`) and the global facet tag set == {1, 231, 232, 233, 234};
- (C5) azimuths.

Every count and integral is reduced (`allreduce`) before it is asserted.

**Negative controls (asserted, with their separations).**
- **(n-absent)** wrong name: `step_name = "PortSheet9"` raises `ValueError` on every rank, naming `PortSheet9` and listing the names present (`step_import.py:410–415`). It is pinned with `match="no dim-2 entity named 'PortSheet9'"`. Separation: a raise against a tagged face of area 0.5·dx·dz to `STRIP_BAND`.
- **(n-hDO)** default read: with `highest_dim_only` removed, the free faces are dropped and the read raises `"no dim-2 entity named 'PortSheet1'"`. Narrowed as above, so the control can no longer pass on an unrelated error.
- **(n-split)** the 993 interface, reproduced. `STEP_HALVES` goes in with 1a's volume groups (loaded from `geo34_fsmall_birdcage_step.json`) and this config's `surface_groups`. It raises `match="is not embedded in any volume"` on every rank. Separation: the same four strips, only the box split differs, and the outcome flips from raise to tag. This is the measurement that ruling (a) is the cause.
- **(n-drop)** one owned strip facet dropped on one rank misses `STRIP_BAND` (U12′'s (n3)). The ratio is printed as a multiple of `STRIP_BAND`; a single facet of a ≈ 5.6e-05 m² strip is many orders above 1e-9.
- **(n-swap)** `PortSheet2` ↔ `PortSheet3` fails (C5) on exactly {2, 3}.

**Printed.** (Q) has two columns. The import column is un-optimised, with no Netgen: cells, min dihedral (degrees), max aspect (defined as max of 1 / (3 r_in / R_circ), from `_tet_quality`, `tests/mesh/probe_wf6_sheet_facet_census.py:83–110`) and the count of tets under 1°. The primitive column is `tests/mesh/test_birdcage_leg_offset._build(np.zeros(LEG_COUNT), leg_sheet_strip_fraction=0.5)`, Netgen on, 117 206-cell record, with the same quantities. The definitions go in the line, and minima / maxima are reduced with `allreduce(MIN/MAX)`. Also printed: `n_triangles` per sheet; the count of `BOPAlgo_AlertIntersectionOfPairOfShapesFailed` alerts (993 saw 68, W1a `:50`; whole vs halves side by side, not gated); and stage seconds (author, import + tag, quality pass).

**Windows.** All windows are bare and foreground, with Bash `timeout: 660000`. `docker compose -f docker/docker-compose.yml exec -T fem-em-solver pgrep -c python3` reads 0 first. Container-side is ≤ 600 s, and `timeout -k 30` is mandatory. These are 993's command forms, changed only where marked:
1. **W0a′** `WF-16-U13a-W0a-positions-whole` (≈ 55–65 s: two builds at W0a's 25.18 s build + export each, against W0a's 28 s window):
   `scripts/testing/run_and_log.sh WF-16-U13a-W0a-positions-whole "docker compose exec -T fem-em-solver bash -lc 'cd /workspace && PYTHONPATH=/workspace/src:/workspace timeout -k 30 120 python3 -u tests/mesh/probe_u13a_fsmall_sheet_positions.py /workspace/logs/u13a_prime'"`
   Changed: the argument is now the output directory. The probe writes both generator STEPs into it.
2. **W0b′** `WF-16-U13a-W0b-author-whole` (≈ 14 s: W0b measured 7 s for one author pass):
   `scripts/testing/run_and_log.sh WF-16-U13a-W0b-author-whole "docker compose exec -T fem-em-solver bash -lc 'cd /workspace && timeout -k 30 120 cq-python tests/mesh/cq_author_u13a_fsmall_four_port.py /workspace/logs/u13a_prime/u13a_fsmall_four_port_whole.step /workspace/logs/u13a_prime/generator_fsmall_whole.step && timeout -k 30 120 cq-python tests/mesh/cq_author_u13a_fsmall_four_port.py /workspace/logs/u13a_prime/u13a_fsmall_four_port_halves.step /workspace/logs/u13a_prime/generator_fsmall_halves.step'"`
   Changed: the output goes to the logs tree, not `tests/mesh/data/`, and both generator paths are explicit.
3. **W1a / W1b / W1c** `WF-16-U13a-W1-n1` / `-n2` / `-n4`, one width per window. Each is estimated at ≈ 120–160 s, against 993's 51 s W1a, which held only fail-fast imports (≈ 10 s each by arithmetic). Built import + tag ≈ 37 s (GEO-34 step 2's stage seconds, less Netgen), primitive `_build` ≈ 25 s (W0a), n-swap a second full import ≈ 37 s, three fail-fast controls ≈ 30 s.
   `scripts/testing/run_and_log.sh WF-16-U13a-W1-n1 "docker compose exec -T fem-em-solver bash -lc 'cd /workspace && PYTHONPATH=/workspace/src:/workspace U13A_STEP_SHA256=<sha from W0b′ whole line> timeout -k 30 180 mpiexec -n 1 python3 -m pytest tests/mesh/test_step_import_u13a_fsmall.py tests/environment/test_complex_mode.py -v -s --tb=short'"`
   W1b / W1c use the same command with `-n 2` / `-n 4` and without the complex-mode file. Changed: only the `U13A_STEP_SHA256=` assignment, copied from W0b′'s `[author] wrote …_whole.step … sha256=` line and cited by `log:line` in the Outcome. It is the same value in all three windows.

**Rule (i):** no test, config or STEP edit after its last window. The STEP is never regenerated between W1a and W1c; the sha pin enforces it.

**Price / Tier / Class / Cost.**
- **Standard**, `-n 1 / 2 / 4`, real build (no complex source), no solve.
- Windows: W0a′ ≈ 1 min, W0b′ ≈ 15 s, and W1a–c ≈ 2–2.7 min each. The total is ≈ 8–10 min of windows.
- An overrun of W1's 180 s is **killed and reported, never re-run with a larger timeout** (CLAUDE.md). The review then splits n-swap into its own window.
- **Class:** chain step (no solve). **Cost:** ≈ 6.0 measured (chain no-solve median, recomputed at the 18:00 11-15 review) / **35 predicted** min. That is four parked files edited, not three written, and no committed STEP.

**Traps already paid for.**
- **The 993 interface trap.** A strip on a face shared by two port-box halves is a boundary face, not an embedded one, and U12′'s lineage rejects it (W1a `:102`). Only `_build(False, …)` solids are this item's input. The halves file feeds (n-split) alone.
- **Stale inputs on disk.** 993's `tests/mesh/data/u13a_fsmall_four_port.step` and `/workspace/logs/u13a_generator_fsmall.step` are halves files, ignored by `.gitignore:57`. The new paths are distinct names under `/workspace/logs/u13a_prime/`, and the sha pin catches a wrong read. Do not delete the stale files and do not `git add -f` any STEP.
- **`cq-python` clears `PYTHONPATH`** (`docker/Dockerfile:41`). The author script cannot import `fem_em_solver`. Its literals are restated with W0a `log:line` provenance, and W0a′'s pre-gate re-confirms them.
- **Path checkout takes code only.** Check out the four parked code paths. Never check out `docs/testing/test-results.md`, `attempts.md` or a chunk file from the branch (`8d4cf58` dropped nine rows). Append record rows by hand, and `git diff --stat` on record files shows insertions only.
- **No `src/` edit.** Option (b) is the weekly's. **Never put this in a slot with another item editing `io/step_import.py`.**
- **The Netgen workaround stays as it is.** Leave the skip at `step_import.py:320–330` alone. Never re-enable `optimize("Netgen")` on the named-sheet branch and never touch the key-absent path (known-issues 2026-11-14 "Never by …"). Item 996 shows that the arm-E topology this item uses SIGSEGVs Netgen at every h.
- **A negative control must match the error it means.** 993's n-hDO `match="PortSheet1"` passed on the embed error. Every `match=` carries the message's distinguishing clause.
- Never `makePlane`; use explicit 4-corner polygon faces. The OCC bbox pads by ≈ 1e-7, so bbox checks are at 1e-6 and area at 1e-12 (`cq_author_geo34_step1d.py:38–41`).
- `highest_dim_only: false` is required. Embedding is read with `getEmbedded`, not upward adjacency (`b8214906`).
- **Exact rectangles only, κ off** (CLAUDE.md hard rule; 2026-10-02 ruling).
- `cell_tags.values`, `assemble_scalar` and local max/min are rank-local: reduce before asserting.
- A gmsh throw on rank 0 under `mpiexec -n 2` can deadlock, so W0a′ / W0b′ run as plain `python3` / `cq-python`. The W1 controls rely on `import_step` raising on every rank, which U12′'s n1 control showed at `-n 1 / 2 / 4`.
- Cell counts are printed, never gated (1b: "mesh differs").
- The harness call is the whole Bash command: no `cd … &&` prefix, no pipe, no suffix (bash_guard). The `&&` between W0b′'s two runs sits inside the container string, as `cd /workspace &&` always has.

**Scope.** This item covers the named-sheet route on F-small with whole port boxes: four exact strips tagged by name with fragment lineage, areas at `STRIP_BAND`, embedded in one volume each, at three widths; and one quality reading. **Not claimed:**
- **U13b, the `Study` solve on this mesh, which is not this item.** Note for U13b's brief: the package's sheet geometry finds a port sheet as the `(port_lower + i, port_upper + i)` interface (`src/fem_em_solver/ports/_sheet_geometry.py:93–97`). A whole-box mesh has no such interface, only facet tag `230 + i`. How U13b's `Study` reads its port is U13b's question.
- any port, S or Z quantity;
- item 192's `P1`;
- that the un-optimised mesh is adequate for a solve;
- the Netgen SEGV's cause (item 996 measured it, did not diagnose it);
- option (b).

**Negative result.** The disposition is the same for every case: report the measurement and stop; never move a band, a strip or a selector to rescue a red.
- **The whole-box strip also reads "not embedded in any volume"** (the boundary-edge question above). Record the lineage, the children and `getEmbedded` per volume (printed). Stop, mark BLOCKED, and park on `attempt/WF-16-U13a-*`. Attempt 3's choices are then the review's: option (b) via the weekly, or the 1c full-CadQuery route. Never shrink the strip off the ±z faces: that is no longer the generator's exact rectangle.
- **Other stops at the CAD stage:** W0a′'s pre-gate differs, W0b′ reads 34 solids on the whole file, or (C0) claims ≠ 1 volume. That is a finding about the export. Stop, mark BLOCKED, park.
- **(C1) / (C3) miss at `STRIP_BAND`:** the route does not carry the exact rectangle on a multi-volume model. Stop, mark BLOCKED, park; the review files known-issues.
- **(n-split) does not raise:** the 993 diagnosis is wrong. Report it; the item is BLOCKED even if (C1)–(C5) are green, because the ruling's premise is unmeasured.
- **(Q) degenerate** (min dihedral below ≈ 1° or a zero-volume tet): print it, do not assert. U13b's premise is then the review's call.
- No `PREDICTED RED:` line.
